<<<SUMMARY>>>

DINO | neutral | HF Sinclair is a minority partner contributing $750 million to the joint venture. While the project provides exposure to stable pipeline cash flows, the company's limited ownership stake (approximately 15.1%) results in a more neutral impact compared to the other partners.
